## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall. The route is logical, running from heritage and facts (3, 4) through the brand scale (7) and brand pages to hotel pages. Weaknesses are consequential, though. Body text is very small on the brand, safety, rewards and hotel pages, and the destination map on slide 5 has tiny labels and overlapping dots that would not read when projected. The brand scale grey axis labels have low contrast on navy, and the hotel pages (18 to 22) repeat a template with thin content and a large empty gap under each title.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the colour-coded brand pages with photo collages (10 to 16), the brand scale matrix concept on slide 7, and the strong photographic title slide.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge body copy and pillars on brand, safety, rewards and hotel pages, brighten the slide 7 axis labels, and simplify slide 5 so market clusters and legend are legible.

**What would change the score:** I would cut the hotel pages to a few key properties, put the heading closer to the content on the brand pages, and tighten the long timeline text on slide 3.

_Confidence medium. 23 of 23 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and a short statement that Deutsche Hospitality delights guests with German hospitality worldwide, followed by a logo grid of its brands. It then traces the company's history on a timeline, from the first Steigenberger hotel in 1930 through acquisitions, the Huazhu ownership change and the 2021 rebrand, and presents key facts on hotel count, rooms, countries and turnover. Destination maps show current property locations and planned openings. Next comes a section on the parent Huazhu group, followed by a brand scale matrix that places each brand by price tier and service type. Brand pages follow, each with a photo collage, a one-line positioning and four brand pillars, covering luxury, lifestyle, upscale, midscale and economy brands. A COVID-19 safety section and a loyalty programme overview come before individual hotel pages with key facts, location and benefits. The deck closes with a contact page and a thank-you slide.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Deutsche Hospitality sales presentation have?**

The Deutsche Hospitality sales presentation has 23 slides. It opens with a title slide on slide 1 and closes with a contact page and a thank-you slide, with slide 23 being the thank-you slide.

**What key facts does the Deutsche Hospitality deck give for hotels, rooms, countries and turnover?**

Slide 4 lists 165 hotels, 32.500+ rooms, 19 countries, 10.000+ employees, 3 continents and 770m+ turnover in 2021. The same slide also shows a map of the countries where the company operates.

**What brand pillars does Deutsche Hospitality list for Steigenberger Icons?**

On slide 10, Deutsche Hospitality lists four brand pillars for Steigenberger Icons: tailor-made services, high-end experiences, remarkable rituals and rich heritage. The slide describes the brand as the new luxury brand within the company's portfolio.

**How does the Deutsche Hospitality history timeline begin and what happens in 2019?**

Slide 3 says the Europäischer Hof in Baden-Baden opened in 1930 as the first Steigenberger Hotel. The same timeline notes that in 2019 Deutsche Hospitality was acquired by Huazhu Group Ltd.

**Layout**

A horizontal two-row timeline spans the full width below a left-aligned two-line title at top left (x 4.6%, y 8.8%); seven milestones sit above and seven below a central navy segmented bar at about y 55.4%, each with a year heading, logo or photo, and description, left-aligned at margins of about 4.6% left and 4.2% right. **Visuals**

Horizontal timeline with 14 milestones alternating above and below a central bar of 12 navy rectangles. Each milestone has a navy circular dot (about 1.4% of slide width) on the bar line and a navy vertical stem (about 0.2% wide) leading to its text: top milestones have stems rising up, bottom milestones stems descending. Image 1 (1930): photograph of the Hotel de Europe in Baden-Baden, a large cream-white multi-storey hotel building with a pitched roof and rows of windows, set against green-brown wooded mountains under a pale sky, with the sign HOTEL DE EUROPE across the top of the image; framing is a boxed rectangle with no crop of note, lit by soft daylight, dominant colours cream, green and brown. Image 2 (1987): IntercityHotel wordmark, with Intercity in bold italic and Hotel in regular weight, dark grey. Image 3 (2015): Jaz logo, lowercase bold 'jaz' with 'in the city' beneath, dark grey. Image 4 (2018): MaxX logo, bold 'Max' with a superscript X, dark grey, with 'by DEUTSCHE HOSPITALITY' beneath. Image 5 (2019 Huazhu): Huazhu logo, a dark stylised four-lobed emblem followed by the Chinese characters 华住 and HUAZHU in small caps below, dark grey. Image 7 (2021 Porsche Design): Steigenberger over PORSCHE DESIGN over HOTELS in a thin serif, dark grey. Image 8 (2019 Zleep): Zleep HOTELS logo, bold dark text in a thin-outlined square box with a white fill. Image 10 (2020): REWARDS logo, a circular dark grey H emblem followed by REWARDS in spaced capitals. Image 11 (2021 Icons): Steigenberger Icons crest emblem above STEIGENBERGER ICONS in spaced capitals, dark grey. Footer: Deutsche Hospitality logo (dark mark with DEUTSCHE HOSPITALITY in small white capitals) on the navy band.

**Layout**

Two-column information layout on a full-bleed map: a large bold title 'The / Facts' sits top-left at about 4.6% from the left and 8.8% from the top, with a left column of four stacked stat blocks (icon, figure, label) and a second short column of two stat blocks beside it. A navy footer bar spans the bottom 8.4% of the slide with the logo at its left and a small page label at its right; the map fills the right and centre of the slide behind the text. Text is left-aligned throughout.

**Visuals**

Light grey (#F5F5F5) world map of Europe, Africa, the Middle East and Asia (cropped at the top and right edges), with a navy (#0C2945) highlight on 19 countries: Sweden, Denmark, the Netherlands, Germany, Belgium, Switzerland, Austria, Hungary, Spain, Tunisia, Egypt, Saudi Arabia, Qatar, UAE, Oman, India, China, Thailand and Turkey. Country names are printed in bold labels over or beside the highlights, white on navy fills and navy on the grey map. Six outline icons in navy circles: a hotel building (165 Hotels), a bed (32.500+ Rooms), a location pin (19 Countries), a globe (3 Continents), a people group (10.000+ Employees) and a money bag with euro sign (770m+ Turnover in 2021). Icons are thin single-weight navy line drawings inside circles with navy outlines.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background with the light grey world map image behind the content.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The destination maps use a restrained navy and grey palette with highlighted locations, making a dense network of properties easy to read.
- Review: Body text is very small on the brand, safety, rewards and hotel pages, and the destination map on slide 5 has tiny labels and overlapping dots that would not read when projected.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body copy and pillars on brand, safety, rewards and hotel pages, brighten the slide 7 axis labels, and simplify slide 5 so market clusters and legend are legible.

**Layout**

Full-bleed white slide with a navy footer band across the bottom 8.4 percent. The title 'Destination Map' sits top-left in two stacked lines at about x 4.6 percent, y 9.3 percent. The main map occupies the left 50 percent, with a legend panel top-right and a grid of six small regional inset maps (Hungary, Spain, Sweden, Egypt, India, then Oman, Qatar, Saudi Arabia, United Arab Emirates, Tunisia) in the right half. Left-aligned title, margins about 4.6 percent. The footer holds the logo at bottom-left and the word 'Seite' at bottom-right; no slide number is printed.

**Visuals**

Map of Germany and neighbouring countries with the Danish islands at top, and insets. Map type: geographic pin map with navy land masses (Denmark, Benelux, Switzerland, Austria, Czech Republic, Belgium) and light grey #E7E7E7 for Germany and China. Pins are filled circles: Steigenberger Icons in orange-coral #E8664A, Steigenberger Hotels and Resorts in tan #D9C2A0, Jaz in the City in purple #5B2A86, IntercityHotel in red #E00000, Steigenberger Porsche Design Hotels in black, House of Beats in mint green, MAXX by Deutsche Hospitality in dark green-grey, Zleep Hotels in yellow #FFE600. New openings are hollow rings with a coloured outline of the same hue. Legend has no axes. Inset maps show Hungary, Spain (Madrid, Camp del Mar), Sweden, Egypt with 11 pins, India with one pin at Bangalore, Oman, Qatar, Saudi Arabia, United Arab Emirates and Tunisia, each in a square crop. The China map is a large light grey landmass with pins for Harbin, Jinan, Zhengzhou, Quingdao, Yangzhou, Heifei, Wuhan, Wuxi, Shanghai, Jingdezhen, Nangchang, Chengdu, Chongqing, Changsha, Guilin, Kunming, Gungzhou and Shenzhen. Small hotel photo thumbnails sit behind some pins in Hungary, Egypt, Vienna, Dubai, Riyadh and Shanghai. Footer logo: white stylised 'DH' monogram with 'DEUTSCHE HOSPITALITY' in white capitals.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look. Background white #FFFFFF with a navy footer band #0C2945. Title in Inter Bold, navy #0C2945, two lines, very large (about 5 percent of slide height per line). Country labels in Inter Bold navy #0C2945 on the light map, white #FFFFFF on navy land. City labels in Inter Regular, near-black #231F20. Legend title labels Inter Bold #231F20. Accent colours are the pin colours listed above. Land shapes are flat with no outlines. Light grey panel behind legend #E7E7E7 at 32 percent opacity. No shadows, gradients or corner radii. Inset panels have square corners.

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A full-height navy panel occupies the left 29.9% of the width with the Huazhu logo near its top (about 14% from the top edge, starting 5.1% from the left edge). The right white area starts at 36.1% from the left and holds a small eyebrow line, a two-line bold title, and a grid of statistics in three columns (x about 36.1, 55.4 and 75.3%) and three rows (y about 45.2, 60.8 and 76.0%). Each stat is a large number above a grey label. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

One logo, placed on the navy left panel: a white four-petal rounded diamond emblem to the left of the white Chinese characters 华住 (set in a light serif-like brush style), with the word HUAZHU in thin white capitals beneath. The logo is about 18.6% of the slide width and 12.5% of the slide height, and is not cropped. No charts, photographs, tables or other illustrations. The statistics are typographic only.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #0C2945 across the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF rectangle covering the right 70.1% of the slide. Two-tone look: deep navy #0C2945 panel and background with white #FFFFFF content area. Title and stat numbers are navy #0C2945, Inter Bold (sans-serif, bold weight, no italics, no capitals or letter spacing). Labels are grey #878787 Inter Bold at small size. Eyebrow line 'A member of Huazhu' is navy Inter Bold. Title is 5.0% of slide height, stat numbers 5.0%, labels and eyebrow 2.6%. Flat shapes, no gradients, shadows, corner radii or outlines.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The brand scale matrix places all brands on one grid, so the portfolio structure is understood at a glance.
- Worth keeping: Keep the colour-coded brand pages with photo collages (10 to 16), the brand scale matrix concept on slide 7, and the strong photographic title slide.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge body copy and pillars on brand, safety, rewards and hotel pages, brighten the slide 7 axis labels, and simplify slide 5 so market clusters and legend are legible.

**Text**

The / Brand / Scale / Luxury / Upper / Upscale / Upscale / Upper / Midscale / Midscale / Economy / Lifestyle / Select Service / Full Service / Steigenberger Icons / Steigenberger Porsche Design Hotels / House of Beats / Steigenberger Hotels & Resorts / Jaz in the city / MaxX by Deutsche Hospitality / IntercityHotel / Zleep Hotels

**Layout**

Two-column split: a 2-by-3 photo grid fills the left 39.8% of the slide width, and a solid navy panel fills the right 60.2%. The title 'The Brand Scale' sits top-left of the right panel at about x 46.2%, y 7.9%, left-aligned in three lines. Below it, a brand-positioning matrix occupies roughly x 48.5% to 96% and y 26% to 89%, with vertical axis labels at x about 46% and horizontal category labels along the bottom at y 88%. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Left: a 2 columns by 3 rows grid of six photographs, butted together with no gaps or borders, each cell about 19.9% wide and 33% tall, full-bleed to the slide edges. Top-left: exterior of the Frankfurter Hof, a cream-stone classical facade with tall lit windows, ornate sculpted figures and gold lettering 'FRANKFURTER HOF' across the top, warm evening lighting, brown and gold tones, cropped at top. Top-right: interior of a circular dark lounge, a raised dark round stage with concentric rings and a white back wall, ceiling dome of illuminated white ribbed panels, grey and charcoal tones, shot from a low central angle. Middle-left: a bedroom detail with a mustard-yellow upholstered headboard, green and white pillows with a dark striped pillow, white bedding, green-and-white chevron wallpaper and a small lamp at left, soft daylight, green, mustard and cream tones. Middle-right: close crop of a woman in a cream blouse at a white table, a hand with red nail polish squeezing an orange slice into a glass of ice, light grey background, bright natural light, cream and orange tones. Bottom-left: a sunlit stone-and-glass facade of a building against a blue sky with clouds, a dark awning bearing gold serif lettering 'STEIGENBERGER', lower part dark and out of focus, cropped at bottom. Bottom-right: a cream stone classical facade with a yellow hanging sign with black frame reading 'Zleep HOTELS', blue sky above, bright daylight, cream, yellow and black tones. Right panel: a matrix diagram with thin light-grey horizontal lines at about y 36.8%, 47.3%, 57.8%, 68.2% and 78.7% spanning x 48.5% to 92.2%, and two thin vertical lines at x about 63.1% and 77.7% spanning y 26.4% to 89.1%. Vertical axis labels from top to bottom: Luxury, Upper Upscale, Upscale, Upper Midscale, Midscale, Economy. Horizontal axis labels: Lifestyle, Select Service, Full Service. Logos placed in the matrix: Steigenberger Icons (crest over wordmark, top-right column, Luxury row); Steigenberger Porsche Design Hotels (wordmark with 'PORSCHE DESIGN' in wide letters, Luxury row, right of Icons); House of Beats (stylised B mark above wordmark, Upper Upscale row, Full Service column); Steigenberger Hotels & Resorts (wordmark, Upper Upscale row, far right); Jaz in the city (stylised J and Z with 'in the city', Upscale row, Lifestyle column); MaxX by Deutsche Hospitality (bold MaxX with 'by DEUTSCHE HOSPITALITY' beneath, Upscale row, Select Service/Full Service boundary); IntercityHotel (bold italic 'Intercity' with regular 'Hotel', Upper Midscale row, Select Service column); Zleep Hotels (small square logo with outlined border, Economy row, Lifestyle/Select Service boundary). All logos are white or light grey on navy.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy fill #0C2945 across the whole slide, with the photo grid placed on top of its left portion. Dark navy background with a single flat colour, no gradient. Title in Inter Bold (geometric grotesque sans-serif), white #FFFFFF, about 5% of slide height per line, tight leading, approximately 1.0 line spacing. Axis and category labels in Inter Bold, grey #878787, about 1.67% of slide height, with the vertical labels rotated 90 degrees counter-clockwise. Logos white #FFFFFF and light grey. Grid lines thin, low-contrast blue-grey, about 1 px, colour near #3A5573. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients. Photographs have no borders and touch each other. The Zleep logo sits inside a thin white outlined square.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Safe / Travels / 7 / Event Area / • Event details will be finalized in advance on booking / • Event organizers have the option to purchase / COVID-19 self-tests and provide / it to participants on a voluntary basis / • Food offered for lunch and coffee break (buffet or a la carte) / depends on local legislations / 1 / General Measurements and / precautions in all areas / • In all public areas, distance markings (at least / 1.5 m distance) are implemented by stickers / • All employees are required to wear face masks / • All high-touch surfaces are regularly / disinfected / • Regular ventilation of all public areas / • Disinfection dispensers are placed at public / touch points / 6 / Wellness, Spa & / Gym Facilities / • Regular ventilation of wellness, spa / and gym facilities / • In line with the official national and / local regulations it may / be necessary to close off certain / areas or parts of areas / 2 / Arrival Departure / Digital Check in / & Out / ▪ Credit card terminals should / be operated by guests / themselves (if possible) / ▪ Disinfection of rooms- and / key cards / ▪ When checking in, each / guest must indicate whether / he or she is travelling on / business or privately / 5 / Restaurants / • Opening hours are adapted to the / volume of guests / • Menus are adapted to the number of / employees and volume of guests / • Buffets are again allowed to the / normal extend / • Distance markings and hygiene rules / are observed by an employee / • Disposal gloves area available upon / request / 4 / Rooms / • Regularly disinfection in the rooms / (contact surfaces, door handles etc.) / •   Rooms sufficiently ventilated / 3 / Cooperation Partners / • Diversey as cooperation partner to provide advice and checks / in all hotels to support all measures / • All partner companies are sensitized to the situation / • Additional, regular coordination with partner companies

**Layout**

A full-height navy panel occupies the left quarter of the slide (x 0 to 25.4%) with the title Safe / Travels set top-left inside it at x 4.5%, y 8.7%. The right three-quarters is a free-form radial arrangement: a white circle with a building icon sits at the centre (x 57.7 to 71.1%, y 39.7 to 63.7%), and seven numbered topic blocks (1 to 7) are placed around it with navy connector lines linking each number to its icon or the circle; numbers sit left of their headings, body text sits below headings, all left-aligned. There is no footer, slide number, date or logo visible.

**Visuals**

Seven numbered topic groups, each with a navy line-art icon and an elbow connector ending in a filled navy dot at each end, plus a central white disc holding a building icon. Icons: (a) event icon: a rounded-rectangle screen with three simple person outlines below it, at about x 62.7 y 26.3 w 4.7 h 7.2, linked to block 7 by a line; (b) warning icon: an outlined triangle with exclamation mark, at about x 72.1 y 36.5 w 4.0 h 6.2, linked to block 1 by a vertical line; (c) clock icon: a circle with hands, at about x 78.0 y 49.8 w 1.7 h 3.0, linked to block 2; (d) reception icon: a service bell on a counter with a suitcase below, at x 74.5 y 49.8 w 5.1 h 9.1, navy line art; (e) massage/wellness icon: a person with a seated figure on a treatment bed, at about x 51.5 y 33.6 w 5.0 h 7.8, linked to block 6; (f) restaurant icon: a cloche over a table with two chairs, at about x 49.0 y 50.5 w 4.7 h 6.7, linked to block 5; (g) bed icon: a double bed with two pillows, at about x 55.3 y 67.7 w 4.5 h 5.9, linked to block 4 by an elbow line; (h) group icon: three people outlines, at about x 68.6 y 66.5 w 4.8 h 8.2, linked to block 3. Central building icon: a navy outlined multi-storey hotel with a grid of square windows, a central tall block and side wings, and a door at the bottom, at about x 60.6 y 45.5 w 7.4 h 12.3, drawn inside the white disc. Icons are uniform navy outline strokes, not filled. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a solid navy #0C2945 panel covering the left 25.4% of the slide. Clean flat corporate style. White slide body with a solid navy left panel. Title Safe Travels is white Inter Bold, about 5.0% of slide height per line, two lines. Section numerals are navy Inter Bold about 5.0% of height. Topic headings are navy Inter Bold about 2.6% of height. Body text is navy Inter Regular about 1.67% of height with tight leading (about 2.0% line pitch). Bullets are small navy round dots, except block 2 which uses small navy square bullets. Connector lines are navy, about 2 px stroke, with filled navy round dots (about 1% of slide width) at their ends, square corners with 45-degree diagonal segments. The central disc is white with a faint soft grey shadow/glow around it. No gradients, no corner radii on panels, no visible shadows on other elements.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a dark navy left panel carries all content on a loose grid with margins of about 4.7% left, and a full-height photograph fills the right third from x 66.6% to the right edge. The H Rewards logo sits top-left at about y 7%. Four feature columns sit in a row below it, then a centred-left section heading with a row of six status badges separated by chevrons, then two bottom sections (Member & Status Benefits left, Redemption Possibilities right). No footer, page number or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Top row: four white filled circles (about 10.3% of slide height in diameter) each holding a thin navy line icon: (1) coins with a star and arrows, (2) a hand holding a euro coin, (3) two stacked photo cards with a person portrait, (4) two champagne flutes clinking. Middle row: six white-outlined circles with thin white line icons joined by five white chevrons: star (Star), rosette badge (Silver), trophy (Prestige), medal (Gold), laurel wreath (Platinum), diamond gem (Diamond). Bottom left: three white-outlined circles with navy-stroked white icons: clock/timer (Early Check-In / Late Check-Out), coffee cup with steam (Food & Beverage Discounts), fork and knife (Free Breakfast). Bottom right: four solid white circles with navy line icons: bed (Accommodation Voucher), dining table with cloche (Gourmet Voucher), house with heart (Room Upgrade), wine bottle and glass (Bottle of Sparkling Wine). Photograph: two young people in sunlight on a wooden promenade, a smiling blonde woman piggy-backing on a bearded man in a white t-shirt, grey shorts and sunglasses, with a phone in her hand; blue sky, palm trees and beach umbrellas behind; warm daylight, saturated blue and warm wood tones; full-height, full-bleed to the right edge and bottom and top, not boxed, not cropped by any overlay.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #0C2945 across the whole slide, with the photograph covering the right third. Flat navy background #0C2945 with no gradient or texture. All text white #FFFFFF. Icons and chevrons drawn in white on the navy zone; the bottom-right and top feature circles are solid white #FFFFFF with navy #0C2945 line icons. Typeface: Inter, a neo-grotesque sans-serif; titles and labels Inter Bold, descriptions Inter Regular. Section headings about 2.6% of slide height; feature titles and badge labels about 1.67%; body and small labels about 1.29%. Circles are plain with no shadow or stroke except the outlined status and benefit circles, which have a white stroke about 0.2% of height thick. Chevrons are thin white strokes. No corner radii apart from the circles, no shadows.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Each brand page uses a full-bleed photo collage, a bold title and four short pillars, a repeatable template that keeps the brands consistent.
- Review: The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall.

**Text**

Luxury
Steigenberger
Icons
The Essence of Luxury
Steigenberger Icons is the new brand within the luxury segment of
Deutsche Hospitality. The brand is celebrating luxurious simplicity by
providing customers with an extraordinary experience.
Brand Pillars
•  Tailor-made services
•  High-end experiences
•  Remarkable rituals
•  Rich heritage

**Layout**

Two-column split: a five-photo mosaic fills the left half edge to edge (x 0 to 50%), and the right half is a flat coral panel holding the text. Text is left-aligned on a left edge at about x 54.2% to 54.7%, giving a margin of about 4.5% of width inside the panel. The title block sits in the upper third, with the 'Luxury' kicker above the large 'Steigenberger Icons' headline. The logo lockup sits top right of the panel at about x 85%, y 7.6%. The body text, 'Brand Pillars' heading and two-column bullet list sit in the lower third, with a large empty gap between title and body. No footer, page number or source line.

**Visuals**

Five photographs form a mosaic on the left half, all full-bleed and butted together with no gaps or borders. (1) Top left, 32.1% wide by 33.3% tall: a woman in a red dress and cream ribbed socks with nude pointed heels, shown from the knees down, holding a tan suede shoulder bag, against a plain light grey studio backdrop; soft even daylight, neutral light grey and cream tones with red and tan accents; cropped at the top of the frame. (2) Top, 17.9% wide by 33.3% tall: a looking-down view of a wooden spiral staircase with ornate dark iron balusters, warm honey-coloured timber treads, bright light from above. (3) Middle left, 17.9% wide by 34.7% tall: a window view into a restaurant with a red patterned wall, a red-shaded table lamp, a set table with white linen and a rattan chair, warm red and amber tones. (4) Middle, 32.1% wide by 34.0% tall: close view of a woman in a white button-up shirt with rolled sleeves and cream trousers, holding a round tart topped with fresh raspberries, red nail polish, plain light grey background, soft even light. (5) Bottom, 50% wide by 33.3% tall: lounge interior with a stone fireplace with a lit fire, brass fireplace surround, two pale sage-green velvet sofas with cushions, a glass-top coffee table with brass legs, a grey stool, teal-glazed table lamps with gold shades, and a grey rug on a wooden floor; warm firelight with neutral stone and gold tones. Logo: the Steigenberger Icons crest, a black heraldic shield with a crown and the date 1930 above the wordmark 'STEIGENBERGER' in spaced capitals with 'ICONS' below in serif capitals, all in near-black #120D0E on the coral background.

**Design**

Background is flat coral orange #ED6D46 with no gradient or texture. Headline 'Steigenberger Icons' is Inter Bold in near-black #120D0E, about 5.0% of slide height per line with tight leading. 'Luxury' kicker is Inter Bold about 2.6% of slide height in #120D0E. Body text is Inter Regular about 2.04% of slide height in dark grey #202020 with lead-in 'The Essence of Luxury' in Inter Bold. 'Brand Pillars' is Inter Bold about 2.04% in #202020. Bullet items are Inter Regular about 2.04% in #120D0E; bullet glyphs are Arial-style round dots about 2.44%. Lines are not spaced widely; body line pitch about 3.0% of height. Shapes: no borders, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients. Photos are sharp rectangles with no shadows.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall.

**Text**

Luxury / Lifestyle / Steigenberger / Porsche Design / Hotels / STEIGENBERGER / PORSCHE DESIGN / HOTELS / Steigenberger Porsche Design Hotels / is the only brand that combines the distinctive Porsche Design lifestyle / with the hospitality and service quality of a Steigenberger hotel. Our flair / for design and zeitgeist, combined with the impressive heritage of a 90- / year passion for the hotel industry set us apart. / Brand Pillars / • Design enthusiasm / • Fascinating functionality / • Hyper-personalization / • Luxury lifestyle

**Layout**

A left half of five photographs forms a mosaic that bleeds to the left, top and bottom edges of the slide with no margin. The right half is a flush dark panel with all text left-aligned at about 54.7% of slide width and a left margin of about 4.7%; the logo sits top-right at x 82.8%, y 9.6%. The title block starts at y 9.4% under a subtitle, the intro paragraph and Brand Pillars sit in the lower part of the panel, and the pillars run in two bulleted columns. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Five photographs of hotel interiors and one portrait, arranged as a mosaic. (1) Top-left, 32.1% wide by 33.3% high, from x 0: a dark bar lounge with black curved leather banquettes and dark tables, tall backlit gold-toned wall panels, circular wall niches, and a ceiling of linear warm light strips; dark tones with warm highlights; not cropped, boxed as a mosaic tile. (2) Top-right, 17.9% wide by 33.3% high, from x 32.1: a bar counter with a curved, brushed-metal front and a grey speckled floor, backlit glass-fronted shelves of bottles behind, and a ceiling of small round lights in a dotted pattern; silver and dark tones. (3) Middle-left, 17.9% wide by 34.7% high, from x 0 and y 32.6: a man in a dark zip jacket and dark-tinted sunglasses, smiling and looking back over his shoulder, standing against a grey concrete wall, with the edge of a dark glossy car body in the lower left; daylight, cool grey and black palette. (4) Middle, 32.1% wide by 33.3% high, from x 17.9 and y 33.3: a lobby with tall marble columns, a lit fireplace in the centre, a glass chandelier above, light grey sofas and armchairs with small round side tables on a pale polished floor, floor-to-ceiling windows on the left; warm white, grey and marble tones. (5) Bottom, 50% wide by 33.4% high, from x 0 and y 66.6: a circular lounge with a dark grey ringed floor, curved dark sofas around the perimeter, lit side tables with frosted lamps, vertical ribbed bronze-toned wall panels and a recessed back doorway with light; dark charcoal and warm bronze palette. Photographs are full-bleed to the slide edges where they meet, with no borders, rounded corners, shadows or overlays. Logo: Steigenberger Porsche Design Hotels wordmark in white, three stacked lines, STEIGENBERGER in a serif, PORSCHE DESIGN in wide-spaced sans capitals, HOTELS in a small serif; no icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid charcoal #202020 behind the right panel. Dark charcoal background #202020 with white text throughout. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF; the accent is white. Typeface is Inter (a neo-grotesque sans-serif); the title and subtitle are Inter Bold, body is Inter Regular, and the Brand Pillars heading is Inter Bold. Title line size is about 5.0% of slide height, subtitle and body about 2.0% to 2.6% of slide height. Bullets are small white dots in Arial. No lines, shapes, gradients, shadows or corner radii, apart from the flush photograph edges.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall.

**Text**

Upscale
Steigenberger
Hotels & Resorts
STEIGENBERGER
HOTELS & RESORTS
Tradition steeped in history, curating contemporary culture
Steigenberger Hotels & Resorts stands for a contemporary hotel
industry, innovation and exceptional service. Upscale hotels and modern
houses in large cities and idyllic areas with first-class spa facilities
promise a stay with unforgettable moments of well-being and an
excellent gastronomic offer.
Brand Pillars
• Cultural narrative • Engaging warmth
• Contemporary excellence • Memorable experiences

**Layout**

A two-part slide: a left half of five edge-to-edge photographs in an irregular mosaic (no gaps, no borders) and a right half of flat sand background holding a left-aligned text column starting at x 54.7%. The Upscale kicker and the two-line title sit at the top of the text column, the logo sits top-right at the same top margin, a large empty band fills the middle, and the body copy, Brand Pillars heading and two-column bullet list sit in the lower third, left-aligned to the same edge. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Five photographs form a mosaic on the left half. Top-left (x 0-32.1%, y 0-33.3%): a bed with white layered pillows and a mauve velvet cushion with dark piping, a black pendant wall lamp with warm glow, a dark slatted wood headboard panel with warm uplighting, and a dark wood nightstand. Top-centre (x 32.1-50%, y 0-33.3%): a lounge corner with two grey upholstered armchairs, a round black-rimmed coffee table, a glass-fronted fireplace with visible flames, and sheer pendant shades at the top. Middle-left (x 0-17.9%, y 33.3-67.3%): a cream and wood curved banquette with cushions, a woven wicker lantern holding orange fruit on a white stone base, and a paper lantern shade above. Middle-right (x 17.9-50%, y 33.3-67.3%): a row of set tables with white cups and saucers, cutlery, a long banquette with patterned cushions in ochre and grey, and a pink flower in a vase at the top right. Bottom (x 0-50%, y 66.7-100%): a hotel lobby with pointed arched wall niches, pale stone columns, hanging cream and brass lanterns, potted palms, rattan armchairs around a round table with green fruit, a floor lamp, and a staff member at a reception counter on the right. The photographs are warm, with lighting from windows and warm lamps, dominant colours of cream, beige, wood brown, and white, with touches of mauve and orange. All five are cropped rectangles set edge-to-edge, not full-bleed across the slide, with no overlay or tint. No chart, table, diagram, or icon appears. The Steigenberger logo is the only logo.

**Design**

Background is a flat sand-beige #DDC9A3 covering the whole slide, including behind the photo mosaic. Titles and the Upscale kicker are dark brown-black #382E2C in a bold geometric grotesque sans-serif (Inter Bold), with the main title at about 5.0% of slide height and the kicker at about 2.6%. Body copy is #202020 in Inter Regular at about 2.04% of slide height, with the lead sentence in Inter Bold. Bullet glyphs are #120D0E in Arial-style regular, about 2.44%. The logo is a classic high-contrast serif in spaced capitals, dark #382E2C, with a smaller light grey-blue sub line. The lines have no rules, borders, or shape styles. There are no corner radii, shadows, or gradients. Photos have square corners.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall.

**Text**

Upscale / Lifestyle
House of Beats
Energized by the beat
Unique in its local approach, united by an authentic and holistic lifestyle, House of Beats draws on the local fashion, music, pop and street art and cultural scene. House of Beats is an innovative lifestyle brand with a profitable, flexible and unique concept based on local DNA.
Brand Pillars
• Fashion
• Music
• Art
• Local DNA
HOUSE OF BEATS

**Layout**

Two-column split: a five-photo mosaic fills the left half edge to edge with no gutters or margins, and a text column sits on the mint right half with its left edge at about 54.6% of the slide width. The title block starts near the top at 9.6% height, the logo sits top right, and the body and pillar list are bottom-weighted, with a large empty band between about 20% and 64% of the height. No footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Five black-and-white and colour photographs tiled in a mosaic on the left half, with no gaps or borders. (1) Top-left, 0-32.1% x 0-33.3% y: a man with a short beard and swept hair in a white bathrobe, raising a clear glass of water with his right hand, against a pale grey wall; to his right a grey sandwich-board sign reading 'FRÜHSTÜCK BREAKFAST' with a black left-pointing arrow, on a black stand; black-and-white, bright, even lighting. (2) Top-right, 32.1-50% x 0-33.3% y: a young woman with short brown hair in a black spaghetti-strap dress, eating from a fork with red lipstick, seated at a white table with a plate of red-and-green salad (beef carpaccio style with egg), muted warm colour on a dark grey wood-panel wall; cropped at the top of the head. (3) Left-middle, 0-17.9% x 32.6-67.3% y: a person in a black hoodie with a red bucket hat and blonde hair, bent over DJ decks, with a green sequin backdrop and a grey textured wall; colour, low key, cropped at the bottom edge. (4) Centre-middle, 17.9-50% x 33.3-67.3% y: black-and-white photograph of a woman in a towel turban and a white bathrobe holding a folded newspaper beside a man in a white bathrobe with dark sunglasses leaning on a balcony rail, with a city street and office buildings in the background, bright daylight. (5) Bottom, 0-50% x 66.7-100% y: a bearded man in a white t-shirt leaning on a railing in front of a graffiti wall with large pink painted flower forms, yellow and black marks, and a green-and-red painted panel behind him; warm colour, daylight, full-bleed and cropped at the bottom edge. Overlaps: the photos are placed edge to edge with no overlap.

**Design**

Background: Solid mint green #75EBB8 fill covering the whole slide, with the photo mosaic on its left half. Mint green full-bleed background #75EBB8 with no gradient or texture. Titles and headings are near-black warm charcoal #382E2C (title) and #202020 (body and headings). The bullet glyphs and pillar items use #120D0E. Typeface is Inter (a neo-grotesque sans-serif): 'Upscale / Lifestyle' Inter Bold at 2.6% of slide height, the title 'House of Beats' Inter Bold at 5.0% of slide height over two lines with tight leading, the body Inter Regular at 2.04% of slide height with about 2.6% line pitch, 'Energized by the beat' and 'Brand Pillars' Inter Bold at 2.04%. Bullets use ArialMT at 2.44%. The logo is a black serif-like uppercase wordmark 'HOUSE OF BEATS' with a black stylised B monogram above it, colour near #0A0A0A. Lines and shapes: none; photos are rectangular with square corners and no shadows.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall.

**Text**

Upscale / Lifestyle
Jaz
in the City
Jaz in the city
Feel the Beat
Distinctive in its local approach, united by an authentic lifestyle, Jaz in
the City draws on the local music and cultural scene. Jaz in the City is an
innovative lifestyle brand with a profitable, flexible and unique concept
based on local DNA.
Brand Pillars
• Music
• Local DNA
• Innovation
• Holistic well-being

**Layout**

Two-part split slide: the left half (0 to 50% of width) is a five-photo mosaic of irregular rectangles stacked in three rows, and the right half is a solid purple panel holding all text. Text sits on a left edge at x 54.7%, with the top group (subtitle and title) starting at y 9.6% and the logo top-right at x 88%, y 9.3%. The body block is anchored to the bottom, with the Feel the Beat paragraph at y 64.4% and the Brand Pillars two-column bullet list ending at about y 92%. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Five photographs, all placed as boxed rectangles with square corners and no overlay, forming a mosaic in the left half. (1) Top-left, x 0 y 0 w 32.1 h 33.3: bar and restaurant interior with a yellow-and-red wire-grid ceiling of cubes, a curved orange leather banquette, round wooden-top tables with black chairs, a bulb in the top-left corner, warm amber lighting; dominant colours orange, yellow, dark brown. (2) Top-right, x 32.1 y 0 w 17.9 h 33.3: dark grey wall with a wood-fronted shelf holding a row of vinyl record sleeves, a turntable and a black hi-fi unit on a walnut sideboard, headphones resting on the sideboard; dominant colours charcoal, walnut brown, red and blue sleeve colours. (3) Left-middle, x 0 y 32.6 w 17.9 h 34.7: a young woman with long dark hair lying on a white bed among pink, teal, yellow and purple confetti, blowing a kiss, with a grey curtain behind; dominant colours white, grey, confetti pastels. (4) Centre-middle, x 17.9 y 33.3 w 32.1 h 34.0: a dark music room with posters and framed concert flyers on the walls, a purple neon sign reading music was my first love, a black ceiling with linear light strips, a glossy dark floor, and tall brass-coloured stands; dominant colours violet, magenta, black, grey. (5) Bottom, x 0 y 66.7 w 50.0 h 33.3: a hotel bedroom with a white bed, white pillows and an orange-brown runner across the duvet, a wood-panel wall carrying a large black-and-white painted portrait of a woman's face with a large eye, a dark corridor and a wooden desk with a tablet and keyboard at lower left; dominant colours white, orange-brown, dark wood, grey. Lighting is mostly warm interior light with purple accents in image 4.

**Design**

Background: Solid purple #5B118A covering the entire slide. Purple background #5B118A throughout, with the photo mosaic on its left half. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF; there are no separate accent colours. Typefaces: Inter, a neo-grotesque sans-serif; bold for the subtitle, title, Feel the Beat and Brand Pillars, regular for body and bullets. The bullet glyph is ArialMT. Title 'in the City' and 'Jaz' are set at 5.0% of slide height; subtitle at 2.6%; body and bullets at 2.04%. Line spacing for the body paragraph is about 3.0% of slide height (about 1.5 times the text size). Shapes: none apart from the rectangular photos and the full-slide fill; no shadows, gradients, corner radii or rules.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall.

**Text**

Midscale
MAXX
by Deutsche
Hospitality
MAXXimise your stay
MAXX is charismatic, creative and focuses on the essentials. MAXX
adapts flexibly to every location but always remains true to itself. The
brand also constantly surprises with its attention to detail, which brings a
smile to the guest’s face.
Brand Pillars
• MAXXimize your connections
• MAXXimize your experience
• MAXXimize your essentials
• MAXXimize your comfort

**Layout**

Two-zone split slide. The left half, from x 0 to 50, is a mosaic of five photographs in a staggered arrangement: a top row of two photos (x 0 to 32.1 and x 32.1 to 50, height 33.3), a middle row of two photos (x 0 to 17.9 and x 17.9 to 50, y 32.6 to 66.7), and a full-width bottom photo (x 0 to 50, y 66.7 to 100). The right half, from x 50 to 100, is a solid green panel holding the text column left-aligned at x 54.7 with a left margin of about 4.7 percent, and the logo at top right (x 82.9, y 9.6). The title block sits at the top of the panel with 'Midscale' above 'MAXX by Deutsche Hospitality'; the body copy and 'Brand Pillars' list sit in the lower third, with the pillars in two bulleted columns (x 56.6 and x 76.5). There is no footer, no slide number and no date.

**Visuals**

Five photographs and one logo. (1) Top-left photo, x 0 to 32.1, y 0 to 33.3: close-up of a bed with a mustard-yellow upholstered headboard with a scalloped top, set against a mint-green and white chevron wallpaper; two mint-green cushions and two white-and-navy striped cushions lie on white pillows and white bedding. Cropped, full-bleed within its cell, with warm daylight from the left. Dominant colours: mustard yellow, mint green, white, navy. (2) Top-middle photo, x 32.1 to 50, y 0 to 33.3: a dark charcoal woven pendant-style lamp shade on a black tripod floor lamp stand, beside a grey sofa with an olive-green velvet cushion and a white cushion; plain off-white wall. Soft natural light, neutral palette: charcoal, grey, olive, off-white. (3) Middle-left photo, x 0 to 17.9, y 32.6 to 67.3: an olive-green armchair with a cream cushion printed with an anchor emblem and the words POTSDAM around it, a red ottoman behind, and a glass-topped table in the foreground; warm daylight. Dominant colours: olive green, cream, red, warm wood tones. (4) Middle photo, x 17.9 to 50, y 33.3 to 67.3: interior of a café or bar with a wall of framed vintage posters and photographs, one framed poster reading VARIETÉ, a tufted tan-leather banquette, dark grey chairs and tan leather chairs around round wooden tables, and small pendant and wall lamps; warm, moody lighting. Dominant colours: brown-grey wall, tan leather, dark grey chairs, green accents. (5) Bottom photo, x 0 to 50, y 66.7 to 100: a hotel double room with a dark navy blue wall and dark navy curtains, a white sheer curtain window, a tall brass floor lamp with a white shade, a grey-white bedspread with white pillows on the right, a small writing desk with a chair in dark teal, a teal upholstered bench, and a yellow-olive armchair in the lower-left foreground; a framed botanical line drawing on the left wall. Full-bleed within its cell, not boxed. Dominant colours: navy, grey-white, teal, mustard yellow. (6) Logo, x 82.9 to 95.4, y 9.6 to 18.7: the MAXX wordmark in bold white sans-serif capitals with the 'X' letters in amber-yellow, the second X underlined by a yellow bar, and the line 'by DEUTSCHE HOSPITALITY' in smaller grey capitals beneath. Sits on the green panel with no box.

**Design**

Background: Solid green #38635A fills the whole slide, with the photo mosaic covering the left half. Two-part layout: a green panel on the right half and a photographic mosaic on the left. The green panel is flat, with no gradient, shadow or border. Title text is white bold sans-serif (Inter Bold) at about 5 percent of slide height for MAXX and by Deutsche Hospitality, with Midscale in white Inter Bold at about 2.6 percent. Body text is white Inter Regular at about 2.04 percent of slide height, with bold lead-ins for 'MAXXimise your stay' and 'Brand Pillars'. Bullets are white ArialMT at about 2.44 percent. Accent colour is amber-yellow in the logo. Photos are rectangular with hard edges, no corner radius, no shadows, butted together with no gaps. Main green colour #38635A.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear brand system: navy base, a photo collage on every brand page, and a distinct colour block per brand (slides 10 to 16), which makes each brand easy to recall.

**Text**

Midscale / IntercityHotel / IntercityHotel (logo, top right) / My relaxed hub in the city / IntercityHotels are centrally located in the most beautiful cities. The / modern city hotels guarantee comfort for the upper middle class and / high mobility. / Brand Pillars / • Central location with easy / accessibility / • Evolving connectivity and digital / functionality – a place to meet / • Sustainable consciousness

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a five-photo mosaic fills the left half (0 to 50 percent of slide width, full height) with photos in a staggered grid, and a flat sage panel fills the right half holding the text. The title block sits top-left of the text panel at about 9.6 to 19 percent of slide height, with the logo top-right. The body copy and two-column bullet list sit in the lower third (about 65 to 92 percent height), left-aligned at x 54.5 percent. No footer, page number or source line.

**Visuals**

Five photographs, all rectangular and unrounded with no borders, overlays or tints. (1) Top-left, bedroom: a made-up double bed with white and grey bedding, two red cushions (one printed with a small IntercityHotel wordmark), a red upholstered armchair at left, floor-to-ceiling window with grey curtains showing a city skyline with tall towers under daylight, and a grey city street map on the wall behind the bed with a small red pin. Soft daylight, neutral whites and greys with red accents. (2) Top-centre-right, reception: a smiling woman with shoulder-length blonde hair in a beige blazer, pink top and leopard-print scarf, seated at a light wood counter holding a phone, with a black laptop open in front, and a blurred green plant and shelving behind. Warm light, medium close framing. (3) Middle-left, dining table: a set table with a potted green plant in a metal pot, two wine glasses, salt and pepper shakers, a folded napkin and cutlery on a wooden table, warm interior light. Cropped at the left and right edges. (4) Middle-centre, lounge: a dark-haired woman in a green satin robe smiling and holding a cup, a smiling man in a grey sweater and a blonde woman in a white jacket holding a glass of beer and a wine glass, seated on cream sofas and a red armchair beside a glass-fronted fireplace with orange flames and a candle lantern on a low table; warm amber lighting. (5) Bottom, breakfast: a woman in a light blue top at left in profile, a smiling man in a navy polo and watch and a blonde woman in cream jacket raising glasses of orange juice at a table laden with plates, pastries, coffee cups and flowers, with windows and restaurant booths behind; soft natural daylight. Dominant colours across the mosaic: red, grey, cream and warm wood tones.

**Design**

Background: Solid sage green #9CAEA9 fill covering the slide, with the photo mosaic placed over its left half.

### Slide 17

**Text**

Economy / Lifestyle
Zleep
Hotels
Zleep Hotels logo
A great night’s zleep.
With Zleep Hotels you feel more than understood. A modern hotel
experience is within your reach – and way better than expected. No
complicated booking, no extra fees, no overwhelming options to pick
from. Everything is just right – like the world-class mattress in your bed.
And surprisingly you’re getting a great night’s zleep, better than ever
before.
Brand Pillars
• The room - focus on a good night’s Zleep
• Check-in - the classic reception does not exist
• Breakfast - always a good and healthy breakfast buffet
• Location - proximity to the actual destination

**Layout**

Two-part split slide: a full-height photographic mosaic fills the left half (x 0 to 50%) and a solid yellow panel fills the right half (x 50 to 100%). In the right panel, the eyebrow and title sit top-left at x 54.7%, the boxed logo sits top-right at x 87.9% to 95.4%, the body copy and a two-column bullet list sit lower-left in the panel with a left margin of about 4.7% of slide width; there is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Five photographs and one logo, arranged as a mosaic on the left half with no gaps. Top-left (0 to 32.1% wide, 0 to 33.3% high): close-up of an illuminated cream script neon sign reading 'check in' (partly cropped at the right edge) above dark wooden slats, black and dark-brown dominant tones, warm light on the lettering. Top-middle (32.1 to 50% wide, 0 to 33.3% high): close-up of a dark grey sofa with a cream macramé-knotted cushion and a pale blue-grey cushion, a teal-green glass sphere vase at right, a white marble table top in the foreground, soft daylight from a window at top, cropped. Middle-left (0 to 17.9% wide, 32.6 to 67.3% high): round light-wood dining table with a black tray holding a glass vase of orange dried branches, cream-upholstered chairs and tan leather chairs behind, a black pendant lamp at top, warm interior lighting, vertical wooden slat partition in background, cropped at edges. Middle (17.9 to 50% wide, 33.3 to 67.3% high): lounge area with tan leather armchair in foreground, grey lounge chair with black metal legs, white round coffee table on a blue-grey rug, a bar counter with black stools in the background, black track lights on a dark ceiling, a grey brick wall with the word KITCHEN in backlit letters and an oak counter with a coffee machine at right; warm amber and grey palette. Bottom (0 to 50% wide, 66.7 to 100% high): double bed with white pillows and white duvet, dark charcoal headboard panel with a black rectangular shelf, a black-and-white photo mural of an industrial hall with exposed ceiling trusses behind the bed, a warm wall light at left, a white door at right, cropped at the bottom and left edges, warm light and dark grey tones. Logo (87.9 to 95.4% wide, 9.6 to 22.9% high): the Zleep Hotels logo, the word Zleep in dark lowercase with HOTELS in widely spaced capitals beneath, centred inside a thin dark-outline square on the yellow background.

**Design**

Background: Solid yellow #FFED00 covering the whole slide, with photographs placed over the left half. Bold, flat, high-contrast look: a saturated yellow field with near-black type, sans-serif throughout (Inter family). Title text is Inter Bold, body text Inter Regular, labels Inter Bold; title size is about 5.0% of slide height, body about 2.04%, eyebrow about 2.6%. Colours: background yellow #FFED00; titles #202020; eyebrow and bullet text #120D0E; body text #202020; accent is the same near-black with no separate accent colour. Logo outline is thin #120D0E, about 0.1% stroke, square corners. Photographs are square-cornered with no shadows, no gradients and no borders, butted edge to edge.

**Layout**

Two-zone slide: a navy text panel fills the left half (about 0 to 49.6% of the width) and a mosaic of five photographs fills the right half edge to edge with no margins or gaps. In the text panel, a small 'Germany' kicker and large title sit top-left with the logo top-right of the panel, then a 'Key Facts' block in two bullet columns, then a 'Location' and 'Benefits' block in two columns at the bottom, separated by two thin horizontal rules. Left margin about 4.7% of the width, all text left-aligned, no footer, no slide number.

**Visuals**

Five rectangular photographs, butt-joined in a mosaic on the right half, plus one logo image. (1) Top-left of mosaic, x49.6 y0 w32.5 h33.4: hotel lobby; a man in dark clothing walks past a glass luggage trolley, brown leather sofa, tall white ceramic floor lamp, glass-walled stair and a lit doorway behind; warm amber artificial light; dominant colours bronze, brown and dark charcoal; full-bleed, not cropped at its edges beyond the frame. (2) Top-right, x82.1 y0 w17.9 h33.4: spa or lounge with light oak wooden daybeds with grey cushions and blue pillows on a wood floor, sheer grey curtains, warm strip light along the floor; dominant oak, grey and blue. (3) Middle-left, x49.6 y33.3 w18.3 h33.3: restaurant; a woven copper-wire pendant lamp hangs over round tables with blue upholstered chairs and place settings, a wire-mesh wall behind with daylight; dominant copper, blue and pale grey. (4) Middle-right, x67.9 y33.3 w32.1 h33.3: guest bedroom; a made bed with white linen, grey throw and blue cushions, a wall-mounted flat-screen TV, a white wall lamp, three small framed pictures on a grey wall, a mosaic-tile panel at right; soft daylight; dominant white, grey and blue. (5) Bottom, x49.6 y66.3 w50.4 h33.7: meeting room; long oak table with dark grey upholstered chairs, white place mats, glasses and carafes, a sideboard with drinks and snacks along the right wall, large wall-mounted screen, a row of ceiling spotlights, window blinds at left; warm lighting; dominant oak, dark grey and warm amber. Logo image x30.7 y3.6 w14.6 h17.3: wordmark STEIGENBERGER over HOTELS & RESORTS in white serif capitals, no box or fill.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #0C2945 across the full slide, behind the text panel. Dark navy background #0C2945 with white text (#FFFFFF) and thin slate-grey rules (about #3E5A73). Typeface is a neo-grotesque sans-serif resembling Inter: Bold for the title, kicker and section headings, Regular for bullets. Title text is about 5.0% of slide height (cap height about 3.5%), section headings about 2.6%, body bullets about 2.04%. Shapes are plain rectangles with no corner radius, no shadows and no gradients. Bullets are small round dots set in Arial. The logo is a white serif wordmark.

Key Facts is a two-column bullet list; Location and Benefits are two columns below the second rule. There is no footer, page number or source line.

**Visuals**

Five photographs and one logo, no charts or diagrams. (1) Top-left of mosaic, aerial daytime view of the Steigenberger hotel: a white multi-storey hotel with a dark grey roof and a round domed gazebo, an outdoor pool with a blue water surface, white sun loungers on green lawn, dense green trees in foreground, a green meadow and farmland in the background; full colour, bright daylight, boxed flush against neighbouring photos. (2) Top-right of mosaic, interior dining room: several grey upholstered chairs around round tables, a row of high bar stools at a counter, a chandelier at top left, framed pictures on a dark wall, herringbone wood floor, warm beige and grey tones, boxed. (3) Middle-left of mosaic, bright atrium with a tall white arched wall, a round window with trees visible, a large sphere-like chandelier, red-painted columns, white cocktail tables with glassware and flowers, cream and white tones, boxed. (4) Middle-right of mosaic, hotel suite: a dark green sofa with cushions, a patterned rug, a round side table with wine glass, a grey upholstered armchair, a tall floor lamp and table lamps, green curtains, a bed with grey throw at right, warm lamp lighting, olive and grey tones, boxed. (5) Bottom band across full mosaic width, outdoor pool: rectangular turquoise pool with diving board and steps, white parasol umbrellas, grey sun loungers on grey paving, green lawn and dark trees behind, bright daylight, full colour, boxed, cropped at the bottom edge. Logo: the Steigenberger Hotels & Resorts wordmark, white serif capitals 'STEIGENBERGER' over 'HOTELS & RESORTS', placed on the navy panel top-right, with transparent padding.

**Design**

Background is solid navy #0C2945 on the left panel. Titles are white #FFFFFF in Inter Bold (geometric sans-serif, weight 700). Body text is white #FFFFFF in Inter Regular (sans-serif, weight 400). Section headings are Inter Bold. Accent colour: none; dividers are thin grey-blue #4A5E72 lines about 0.2% of slide height. Title is about 5.0% of slide height, subtitle 2.6%, section headings 2.4%, body and bullets 2.04%. Bullets are small white round dots. Photos are sharp-cornered rectangles with no shadows, borders or gradients; no corner radii.

• FreeCityTicket for H Rewards members

**Layout**

Two-zone slide: a full-height navy panel on the left (about 49.6% of width) holds all text, and a five-photo collage fills the right half in a three-row grid with tight gutters. Left text is left-aligned at a margin of about 4.7% from the left edge; 'Germany' and the title sit at the top, with a logo at top right of the panel. Two thin horizontal rules divide the panel at about 38% and 66% of height, separating Key Facts from Location and Benefits, which sit in two columns. No slide number or footer is visible.

**Visuals**

Five photographs and one logo. (1) Top-left photo, box x 49.6 y 0.0 w 32.5 h 33.4: a hotel lounge with a tall open wooden-and-metal shelving partition, a tan sofa, brown pouf stools, a light-green armchair, shelves with books and orange flowers, and bright white window curtains; warm beige, tan and green tones; full-bleed in its cell, no visible overlay. (2) Top-right photo, box x 82.1 y 0.0 w 17.9 h 33.4: a conference room with grey upholstered chairs around a long light wooden table with water glasses, brown curtains on the back wall, and a partial 'IntercityHotel' sign at top left; grey and brown tones. (3) Middle-left photo, box x 49.6 y 33.3 w 18.3 h 33.3: a seating corner with a bright green moss wall panel at top left, a red wingback armchair, a brown leather armchair, a tall pendant-style floor lamp, a glass bulb lamp on a round wooden side table and wooden stools; warm red, brown and green tones. (4) Middle-right photo, box x 67.9 y 33.3 w 32.1 h 33.3: a double hotel bedroom with white pillows and a striped navy and white bedspread, two purple upholstered chairs, a wooden desk and a window with a view of trees and buildings, navy curtains and wall sconce lamps; cream, navy and purple tones. (5) Bottom photo, box x 49.6 y 66.3 w 50.4 h 33.7: a restaurant with red pendant rings hanging from the ceiling, a long table with rows of multicoloured chairs (red, orange, green, magenta), a plant in a white pot, a small table with salt and pepper shakers and checked placemats, and beige walls; warm red, orange and green tones. Logo: the IntercityHotel wordmark, 'Intercity' in bold italic and 'Hotel' in light regular, white, at top right of the navy panel; no tint.

**Design**

### Slide 22

**Text**

Germany
IntercityHotel
Augsburg
Key Facts:

• Barrier-free rooms
• Breakfast restaurant, BistroBox
Location:
Benefits:

• City centre 1 km / 0,6 mi
• Fair 5 km / 3 mi
• Airport (MUC): 85 km / 53 mi
• Free WiFi

• FreeCityTicket for H Rewards members

**Layout**

Two-column split slide: a dark navy text panel fills the left half (x 0 to about 49.6%) and a five-photo mosaic fills the right half (x 49.6 to 100%) edge to edge with no gutters on the top-right block. Text is left-aligned at a left margin of about 4.7% and the title sits top-left below the country label, with the logo top-right of the panel at about x 30 to 45%. Two thin horizontal rules split the panel into three bands (heading and title band, Key Facts band, Location and Benefits band), and the Location and Benefits lists form two columns starting at x 4.7% and x 25.5%. There is no footer, page number or date.

**Visuals**

Five photographs and one logo image, all unboxed rectangles with square corners and no overlay. (1) Top-left of the mosaic, x 49.6 to 82.1%, y 0 to 33.4%: a hotel lounge with a red-orange wall, three grey upholstered armchairs around a round glass-top side table with a single white vase and green flower, a black drum floor lamp at right, a potted green plant and flower arrangement at upper left, dark carpet, warm even interior lighting, dominant colours red-orange, grey and black; the photo is cropped at the frame edges. (2) Top-right, x 82.1 to 100%, y 0 to 33.4%: exterior of the hotel building with a pale grey-blue facade, a curved glass wing with dark teal window frames and red mullions, a red 'IntercityHotel' sign partly cut off at the left edge, blue sky, daylight; cropped at the right edge. (3) Middle-left, x 49.6 to 67.9%, y 33.3 to 66.6%: a bedroom with two white pillows and white duvet on a light wood bed, a blue-violet curtain, a framed abstract picture on the left wall, a small wall-mounted cabinet, soft daylight, dominant colours white, beige and blue. (4) Middle-right, x 67.9 to 100%, y 33.3 to 66.6%: a meeting room with a long dark table set with glasses, bottles and green apples, dark teal upholstered chairs along the left, a white wall-mounted projection screen with a red 'IntercityHotel' logo, large windows on the right with city buildings outside, beige walls, ceiling spotlights; dominant colours beige, white, dark teal and grey. (5) Bottom band, x 49.6 to 100%, y 66.3 to 100%: a breakfast restaurant with an orange leather banquette at left, dark wood round and square tables with white cups, saucers and small vases, dark wood chairs with grey-green seat cushions, green plants in a tall planter, large windows with trees and buildings outside, patterned green-and-beige carpet; dominant colours orange, dark brown and green; cropped at the bottom and right edges. (6) Logo, x 30.2 to 44.8%, y 2.9 to 20.2%: the white 'IntercityHotel' wordmark, where 'Intercity' is bold italic and 'Hotel' is regular upright, in a 292 by 194 pixel image with transparent surround.

**Design**

Background is a solid deep navy #0C2945 across the whole slide, including the photo area behind the mosaic. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF; accent colour is the same white. Thin divider rules are a muted slate blue-grey #4E6479 at about 1 pixel. Typeface is Inter (a neo-grotesque sans-serif): title and headings in Inter Bold, body in Inter Regular, bullets in Arial. Title 'IntercityHotel Augsburg' is about 5.0% of slide height per line with a cap height of about 3.5% (about 0.7 of the size). Headings 'Germany', 'Key Facts:', 'Location:' and 'Benefits:' are about 2.6% of slide height and Bold; body list text is about 2.04% of slide height in Regular. Line spacing about 3.5 to 4.0% of slide height between list items. Shapes: two horizontal rules only, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a full-height photograph fills the left 50% of the slide, and a solid navy panel fills the right 50% with all text left-aligned at about x 54.6%. The Chinese heading sits at the top of the right panel, the English title below it, then the logo, address, social icons, contact lines, and a partner-logo strip near the bottom. There is no page number, footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph in the left half: an interior lounge detail. In the foreground a beige upholstered armchair with a brass-coloured metal frame angles from lower left; behind it a pale glass-topped shelf holds a hammered copper-gold bowl and a tall clear glass cylinder with a brass rim, next to a navy cylinder with a brass base. Above, white branch-like sculptural wire hangs from the top edge, and the back wall is dark navy vertical slats with gold-brass rectangular panels. Lighting is soft and warm on the upholstery and metals, with the navy wall in shadow. Dominant colours are navy, beige, brass and gold. The photo is full height, boxed to the left half, not full-bleed across the slide, with no overlay or tint. Right panel: a white Deutsche Hospitality logo (D H monogram plus DEUTSCHE / HOSPITALITY wordmark). Row of six white icons: Facebook, Twitter, YouTube, Instagram, LinkedIn, Xing. Two thin grey horizontal rules frame a row of eight white partner logos: Steigenberger Icons, Steigenberger Porsche Design Hotels, Steigenberger Hotels & Resorts, House of Beats (B mark), Jaz in the City, MaxX by Steigenberger Hotels, IntercityHotel and Zleep Hotels.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #0C2945 across the whole slide, with the photograph covering the left half. Solid deep navy background #0C2945 on the right panel, with the photograph on the left. All text is white #FFFFFF. The Chinese heading uses a light weight of MicrosoftJhengHei at about 5.9% of slide height. The English title is Inter Bold, sans-serif, about 5.0% of slide height, two lines. Body and contact text is Inter Regular at about 1.7% of slide height, with bold labels Tel and Web.
